We further validate its generalization on RealEstate10k, highlighting its applicability to real-world data.

</details>


### [147] [WorldReward: Reward Modeling for Camera-Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03952)
*Yibin Wang,Zehan Wang,Junshu Tang,Zhimin Li,Yujie Zhou,Jiazi Bu,Pengyang Ling,Feng Han,Zhixiong Zhang,Long Xing,Shengyuan Ding,Ziang Li,Cheng Jin,Yuhang Zang,Jiaqi Wang,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldReward是一个基于VLM的成对偏好奖励模型，统一了相机条件世界模型中动作一致性和视觉质量的评估。它将成对视频分解为动作对齐的块，组织为结构化视觉证据，并通过投票聚合成视频级偏好。在人类标注基准WorldReward-Bench上，它在动作一致性、外观质量和运动质量三个维度均取得最高的一致性，超过GPT-5.5 3.42、1.45和3.56个百分点。用于RL后训练时可同时提升动作执行和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型分别评估几何和图像质量，无法统一判断动作执行与视觉质量。作者提出VLM可为动作与视觉结果提供共享推理空间，但评估完整长视频及其完整动作序列会产生冗长嘈杂的上下文，导致短暂局部动作证据被稀释或遗漏。

Method: WorldReward将成对视频分解为动作对齐的块，将每个块组织成结构化视觉证据，并通过投票聚合各块决策，分别生成视频级的动作一致性和视觉质量偏好。训练使用大规模推理增强偏好数据集，由前沿VLM生成结构化判断，并经基于工具的多智能体审计和针对性人工审查优化。同时构建人类标注基准WorldReward-Bench。

Result: WorldReward在WorldReward-Bench三个维度（动作一致性、外观质量、运动质量）上与人类偏好的一致性均最高，分别超过GPT-5.5 3.42、1.45和3.56个百分点。用于HY-WorldPlay 1.5的RL后训练时，在短期到长期时间跨度内均持续改善动作执行和视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过将长视频分解为动作对齐的块并聚合决策，WorldReward有效解决了长视频上下文中局部动作证据稀释的问题，实现了动作一致性与视觉质量的统一评估，并在奖励建模和RL后训练中均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Camera-conditioned world models generate interactive videos in which commanded actions should induce the expected scene changes while appearance, geometry, and temporal dynamics remain coherent. Existing rewards assess these requirements separately: geometry-based rewards estimate trajectory execution but cannot judge the visual quality of the executed motion, whereas image-based rewards measure frame quality without capturing action execution or temporal dynamics. We posit that a vision-language model (VLM) offers a shared reasoning space for relating actions to their visual outcomes. However, judging a complete long video against its full action sequence creates a lengthy, noisy context in which short-lived local action evidence can be missed or diluted. We present WorldReward, a VLM-based pairwise preference reward model that unifies action-consistency and visual-quality evaluation for camera-conditioned world models. WorldReward decomposes paired videos into action-aligned chunks, organizes each chunk into structured visual evidence, and aggregates chunk-level decisions by voting into separate video-level action and visual-quality preferences. To train it, we construct a large-scale reasoning-augmented preference dataset using structured judgments generated by a frontier VLM and refined through tool-based agent auditing and targeted human review. We further introduce WorldReward-Bench, a human-annotated benchmark measuring reward-model agreement with human preferences across action consistency, appearance quality, and motion quality. WorldReward achieves the highest agreement on all three dimensions, exceeding GPT-5.5 by 3.42, 1.45, and 3.56 percentage points, respectively. When used for RL post-training of HY-WorldPlay 1.5, it consistently improves both action execution and visual quality across short- to long-term horizons.

</details>


### [148] [RARF: Region-Aware Rectified Flows for 3D Brain MRI Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03956)
*Tomas Guija-Valiente,Blanca Rodriguez-Gonzalez,Norberto Malpica,Angel Torrado-Carvajal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RARF，一个任务无关的区域感知修正流框架，用于掩膜数据生成。针对3D脑MRI修复任务（BraTS Inpainting Challenge 2026），RARF将随机插值过程限制在修复区域内，保留观测体素作为患者特异的解剖上下文。使用掩膜流匹配与重建一致性目标训练三维神经网络，推理时通过学习的速度场将初始噪声传输至缺失组织的合理重建。实验表明该方法在BraTS评估协议下具有竞争力的重建效果并保持解剖一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像修复有望通过重建病理区域内的健康组织来改进自动化脑MRI分析，但现有方法难以在修复缺失区域的同时保持患者特异的解剖一致性。

Method: RARF将随机插值过程约束在修复（inpainting）区域内，观测体素保持不变并提供患者解剖上下文。三维神经网络输入部分缺失图像（缺失区域填充高斯噪声）、修复掩膜及对应时间步，采用掩膜流匹配（masked flow-matching）与重建一致性（reconstruction-consistency）目标联合训练，并配合掩膜感知的预处理和数据增强。推理时利用学习得到的速度场将初始噪声导向合理的组织重建，再与未改变的观测解剖结构拼接。

Result: 在BraTS评估协议下，RARF产生具有竞争力的重建结果，同时保持解剖一致性。

Conclusion: RARF作为一种任务无关的区域感知修正流框架，在3D脑MRI修复任务中验证了其有效性，能够在不依赖具体任务的前提下生成解剖一致的健康组织重建，展现出在自动化脑MRI分析中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Medical image inpainting has the potential to improve automated brain MRI analysis by reconstructing healthy tissue within pathological regions. We introduce RARF, a task-agnostic region-aware rectified flow framework for masked data generation. We instantiate the framework for 3D brain MRI inpainting as our submission to the BraTS Inpainting Challenge 2026. RARF restricts the stochastic interpolation process to the inpainting region, while the observed voxels remain fixed and provide patient-specific anatomical context. A three-dimensional neural network receives the partially voided image, with Gaussian noise filling the missing region, together with the inpainting mask and the corresponding timestep. The model is trained using masked flow-matching and reconstruction-consistency objectives, combined with mask-aware preprocessing and data augmentation. During inference, the learned velocity field transports the initial noise toward a plausible reconstruction of the missing tissue, which is then combined with the unchanged observed anatomy. Experiments under the BraTS evaluation protocol show that the proposed approach produces competitive reconstructions while maintaining anatomical consistency. Source code is available at: https://github.com/TomasGuija/rarf.

</details>


### [149] [Sharpening the Ensemble: An SSIM-Aligned Residual Refiner for Brain-MRI Inpainting Post-Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03981)
*Kubilay Kağan Kömürcü,İlkay Öksüz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级残差精修器（residual refiner）作为后处理阶段，对两个2025年BraTS榜单并列第一的脑MRI修复（inpainting）模型所组成的深度集成模型输出进行二次增强。精修器在L1损失基础上加入结构相似性（SSIM）项，中等的结构性权重λ将集成模型在官方验证集上的SSIM从0.8555提升到0.8572，在保留集上从0.8767提升至0.8780，MSE几乎不变，代价低且无需大规模重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管当前最强脑MRI修复模型在BraTS局部合成基准上表现准确，但多个模型因训练损失中L1和MSE项的平均值寻求（mean-seeking）行为而产生模糊的合成区域。作者希望在不进行大规模重训练的前提下，通过后处理方式缓解这一模糊问题，提升已有强模型的结构质量。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 对两个2025年BraTS并列第一的模型构成深度集成（deep ensemble）；(2) 训练一个轻量级残差精修器，输入为集成模型的输出，在L1损失基础上加入结构相似性项，并对其权重λ进行调参；(3) 通过两个消融实验验证有效性：一是向两模型集成中加入第三个模型会降低性能，二是经典非锐化掩蔽（unsharp masking）在所有强度下均无法提升SSIM，证明增益来自学习式而非盲目的锐化。

Result: 在中等λ下，精修器将SSIM从0.8767提升至0.8780（保留集）及从0.8555提升至0.8572（官方验证排行榜），MSE几乎不变。增益虽小但一致，62.6%的保留集案例得到改善（符号秩检验p=2.2×10⁻⁷）。过度加权结构项会逆转增益。两个消融实验界定效果边界。

Conclusion: 作者提供了一种廉价、可复现的后处理阶段，在不进行大规模重训练的前提下即可改善已很强的集成模型性能。关键发现是：适度的结构相似性权重带来小幅但统计显著的改善，而过度加权或引入第三个模型反而有害，说明效果源于学习到的锐化而非盲目增强。

Abstract: Brain-MRI inpainting replaces a masked region of a scan with synthesized, anatomically plausible healthy tissue, so that analysis tools built for healthy brains can be applied to images they would otherwise reject. On the BraTS local-synthesis benchmark, which ranks submissions on the structural similarity index (SSIM), the peak signal-to-noise ratio, and the mean squared error (MSE) jointly, the strongest recent models are accurate, but several report blurry synthesized regions and attribute this to the mean-seeking behavior of the $\ell_1$ and MSE terms in their training losses. We address this in post-processing, forming a deep ensemble of the two co-first-place 2025 models and training a lightweight residual refiner on the ensemble's own outputs under an $\ell_1$ loss augmented with a structural-similarity term whose weight $λ$ we vary. At a moderate $λ$ the refiner improves SSIM over the ensemble, from $0.8767$ to $0.8780$ on a held-out reproduction of the official scorer and from $0.8555$ to $0.8572$ on the official validation leaderboard, with essentially no change in MSE. The gain is small but consistent, improving $62.6\%$ of the held-out cases with a signed-rank $p=2.2\times10^{-7}$, whereas over-weighting the structural term reverses it. Two ablations bound the effect. Adding any third model to the two-model ensemble degrades it, and classical unsharp masking fails to improve SSIM at any strength (best $0.8765$ against $0.8767$), so the gain reflects learned rather than indiscriminate sharpening. The result is a cheap, reproducible post-processing stage that improves an already strong ensemble without any large-scale retraining.

</details>


### [150] [IchthyoNoma: Nomenclature and Context Sensitivity of Zero-Shot Biological Vision--Language Models for Bangladeshi Freshwater Fish Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03985)
*Nazim-E-Alam,Tarek Rahman,Md Kishor Morol*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了多种零样本视觉-语言模型（CLIP、BioCLIP、BioCLIP2及多语言Jina CLIP v2对照组）在孟加拉国淡水鱼图像识别任务中的表现，揭示准确率并非单纯反映生物视觉知识，而是受生物专精化、多语言对齐、命名法、提示词形式和上下文共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本VLM正日益被用作免训练的物种识别器，但其报告的准确率可能受多种非生物视觉因素干扰，而非真正体现物种知识。作者旨在厘清这些混杂因素，避免对模型生物识别能力的误读。

Method: 作者在两个孟加拉来源（BFF-15和SylFishBD，共10,321张图像，七个淡水鱼类别）上对五种模型（CLIP、BioCLIP、BioCLIP2、Jina CLIP v2）进行审计，并设计配对干预实验（弱模糊、强模糊/灰度掩蔽、白色掩蔽、物种依赖性分析），同时考察英语/科学名/孟加拉语提示词的性能差异。

Result: BioCLIP2在BFF-15英语常用名上达72.36%，在SylFishBD科学名上达68.91%，而通用CLIP仅25.15%和14.40%。BioCLIP2的孟加拉语提示词仅在平衡准确率上接近随机水平（14.22-14.29%）；Jina可将孟加拉语判别部分恢复至21.89%和16.36%，但纯孟加拉语名称又回到14.29%。干预实验表明弱模糊无显著影响，较强模糊/灰度有中等损失，白色掩蔽产生更大伪影，物种依赖性显著。

Conclusion: 零样本生物VLM的得分是生物专精化、多语言对齐、命名法、提示词制定和上下文等多因素共同作用的结果，评估时必须同时控制这些变量，不能简单将准确率等同于模型的视觉物种知识。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as training-free species recognizers, but reported accuracy can reflect more than visual species knowledge. We audit CLIP, BioCLIP, BioCLIP2, and a multilingual Jina CLIP v2 control on seven freshwater-fish categories from two Bangladeshi sources (10,321 images). BioCLIP2 reaches 72.36% on BFF-15 with English common names and 68.91% on SylFishBD with scientific names, versus 25.15% and 14.40% for generic CLIP. BioCLIP2 Bengali prompts are near chance in balanced accuracy (14.22-14.29%); Jina partially recovers Bengali discrimination to 21.89% and 16.36%, but bare Bengali names return to 14.29% on both sources. Paired SylFishBD interventions show no significant weak-blur effect, modest losses from stronger blur/gray masking, a larger white-mask artifact, and strong species dependence. Zero-shot biological VLM scores therefore jointly reflect biological specialization, multilingual alignment, nomenclature, prompt formulation, and context.

</details>


### [151] [Catalogue Photography as a Cold Start: Toward Deployable Carbide Burr Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03995)
*Abilash Philip Madavath,Chandra Yuvesh Aubeeluck,Augustin Raju,Nicolas Pyschny,Felix Hackelöer,Florian Zwanzig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在无标注现场图像条件下，仅用制造商目录照片作为监督来源，验证硬质合金旋转锉等铣削工具批次是否符合生产订单的质量检测任务，量化了目录监督到现场照片的域迁移性能差距，并发现灰度转换与匈牙利匹配等简单改动比模型规模更能提升迁移增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铣削工具/硬质合金旋转锉的批次验证仍依赖人工且易出错的手工质检。用计算机视觉自动化该过程面临冷启动约束——没有标注现场图像可用，目录摄影是唯一的监督来源。作者希望探究目录监督在域偏移下能多大程度支撑工业识别管线。

Method: 作者调查了目录监督对工业识别管线的支持能力，明确测量目录可分离性与现场照片保留集性能之间的差距。方法包括：比较现成冻结特征提取器对两个任务属性（头部形状、齿形轮廓）的分离效果；采用度量学习进行无监督聚类发现；通过灰度图像转换和基于已知订单表的匈牙利分配法约束检索来降低域敏感性。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1) 现成冻结特征提取器无法可靠分离头部形状与齿形轮廓两个属性，需要针对性的表示学习；(2) 度量学习在目录图上产生接近完美的无监督聚类（ARI 0.94-0.97），但该增益只有不到一半转移到现场照片；(3) 最大的迁移增益来自降低域敏感性的简单改动——灰度转换提升+0.22，利用订单表通过匈牙利分配约束检索提升+0.11，而非模型规模或表示复杂度。

Conclusion: 目录摄影应被视为有用的冷启动而非可直接部署的训练域。作者为此提供了经验基线和面向精密工具制造中目录到现场迁移的评估协议。

Abstract: Verifying that manufactured batches of milling tools or carbide rotary burrs conform to production order sheets remains a largely manual and error-prone quality assurance task. Automating this process with computer vision faces a critical cold-start constraint since no labelled imagery is available, leaving manufacturer catalogue photography as the sole source of supervision. We investigate how far catalogue supervision can support an industrial recognition pipeline under domain shift, explicitly measuring the gap between catalogue separability and performance on held-out field photographs. Our findings reveal three key insights. First, off-the-shelf frozen feature extractors do not reliably separate the two task attributes, head shape and tooth profile, motivating targeted representation learning. Second, metric learning produces near-perfect unsupervised cluster discovery on catalogue images (adjusted Rand index 0.94--0.97), but less than half of this gain transfers to field photographs. Third, the largest transfer gains do not come from model scale or representation complexity, but from simple changes that reduce domain sensitivity: converting images to grayscale (+0.22) and constraining retrieval using the known order sheet via Hungarian assignment (+0.11). We therefore treat catalogue photography as a useful cold start rather than a deployment-ready training domain, and provide empirical baselines and an evaluation protocol for catalogue-to-field transfer in precision tool manufacturing.

</details>


### [152] [The Blind Spot in 2D Infants' Pose Estimation:Robust Learning from Noisy Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04009)
*Emanuele Cardinale,Marco Proietti,Alessandro Cacciatore,Maria Francesca Spadea,Lucia Migliorelli,Sara Moccia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出REMIND方法，一种基于聚类和训练动态的关键点选择策略，用于在姿态估计任务中识别并处理噪声标注，特别针对早产儿临床场景中的姿态估计问题。在NeoPose数据集上，REMIND在多种噪声场景下实现了高达93%的AUC，是首个明确解决早产儿姿态估计中标签噪声问题的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声标注会严重损害深度学习模型性能。尽管分类任务中的标签鲁棒性已被广泛研究，但姿态估计场景下的噪声标签问题仍然缺乏探索。在临床早产儿监测这一关键应用中，图像标注面临关键点自遮挡、护理人员干扰等视觉挑战，标注过程天然易出错，因此亟需在姿态估计中解决噪声标注问题。

Method: REMIND（REliable keypoint selection via Memory of traINing Dynamics）是一种基于聚类的关键点选择策略，利用逐关键点的训练动态来识别噪声标签。该方法不假设噪声分布的任何先验知识，通过分析训练过程中的关键点行为来区分可靠与不可靠的关键点标注，从而实现在干净标签子集上的模型训练。

Result: 在NeoPose数据集（包含46名早产儿的46段真实临床录影）上进行评估，REMIND在多种噪声污染场景下均能正确识别噪声标注，并使用三种不同的姿态估计架构实现了高达93%的AUC（Area Under the Curve）。

Conclusion: 这是首个明确针对早产儿姿态估计中的标签噪声问题进行研究的成果，为数据质量无法保证的婴儿监测场景中设计可信赖的基于学习的算法开辟了道路。

Abstract: Noisy annotations pose a significant challenge for supervised deep learning, as neural networks rely on large-scale, high-quality labeled data whose corruption can severely impair model performance. Although robustness to label noise has been extensively studied for classification tasks, it remains relatively underexplored in Pose Estimation (PE). This limitation becomes critical in clinical contexts, including neonatology, where PE of preterm infants is used to support the assessment of spontaneous motility, a key indicator of neurodevelopmental trajectories. In such settings, infants' images labeling is further hindered by visual challenges (e.g., keypoint self-occlusions, caregiver interference), making the annotation process inherently susceptible to errors. To tackle noisy annotations in PE, we introduce REliable keypoint selection via Memory of traINing Dynamics (REMIND), a clustering-based keypoint-selection strategy that exploits keypoint-wise training dynamics to identify noisy labels without assuming any prior knowledge of the noise distribution, thus enabling noise-free model training. When evaluated on the proprietary NeoPose dataset, comprising 46 videos of 46 preterm infants recorded in real clinical settings, REMIND correctly identifies noisy annotations across multiple corruption scenarios, achieving up to 93\% Area Under the Curve (AUC) with three different PE architectures used in the relevant literature. To our knowledge, this is the first study to explicitly address label noise in preterm infants' PE, paving the way for the design of trustworthy learning-based algorithms for infants'monitoring support when data quality cannot be guaranteed.

</details>


### [153] [Stable and Scalable Bundle Adjustment of Holistic 3D Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04026)
*Shaohui Liu,Rémi Pautrat,Daniel Barath,Richard Hartley,Viktor Larsson,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的束调整（BA）框架，该框架能够联合优化几何特征（点、线等）与高阶关系（如共面性、平行性等）。核心思想是将高阶关系组建模为类似相机的实体，使组约束和跨特征关联都能通过2D重投影误差表达，从而在Schur消元下保持经典点BA的稀疏结构，避免3D正则化带来的数值不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统束调整仅针对相机参数和稀疏3D点进行优化，虽已有扩展集成线条等图元，但引入诸如平行性、共面性或线框等更丰富的几何结构往往导致计算成本显著增加且数值稳定性下降，缺乏一个统一且高效的框架来处理几何特征与高阶关系。

Method: 提出一个统一的束调整框架：(1) 引入一种分类法，区分具有直接2D测量的可扩展几何特征（点、线）与编码高阶关系的组（共面性、平行性等）；(2) 将高阶关系组建模为类似相机的实体，使组约束和点-线关联等跨特征关系都能通过2D重投影测量来表达；(3) 构造组诱导与跨特征的重投影误差，在Schur消元下保持经典点BA的稀疏结构，同时避免直接3D正则化导致的病态条件与数值不稳定。

Result: 在真实与合成数据集上的实验表明，该方法在运行时性能上与经典纯点束调整相当，同时能产生显著更丰富的3D结构并提升几何精度。

Conclusion: 通过将高阶几何关系统一建模为类似相机的实体并以2D重投影误差表达约束，所提出的框架在保持经典BA的计算效率与数值稳定性的同时，实现了更丰富的3D结构重建与更高的几何精度，为束调整在复杂几何场景下的扩展提供了统一且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Bundle Adjustment (BA) is a cornerstone of 3D computer vision and has benefited from decades of advances in sparse optimization and numerical methods. It was originally developed for jointly optimizing camera intrinsics, poses and sparse 3D points. While extensions incorporate lines and other primitives, integrating richer geometric structures such as parallelism, coplanarity, or wireframes often introduces significantly increased computational cost and reduced numerical stability. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that extends bundle adjustment to jointly optimize geometric features and higher-order relations. We first introduce a taxonomy that distinguishes scalable geometric features with direct 2D measurements (e.g., points and lines), from groups encoding higher-order relations (e.g., coplanarity, parallelism, etc.), where we show that groups can be modeled as camera-like entities within the bundle adjustment framework. Building on this formulation, we propose that both group constraints and cross-feature relations (i.e., point-line associations) can be expressed through 2D reprojection measurements. By formulating group-induced and cross-feature reprojection errors, we preserve the sparsity structure of classical point-based BA under Schur elimination, while avoiding direct 3D regularization that degrades the conditioning and stability. Experiments on both real-world and synthetic datasets demonstrate runtime performance comparable to classical point-only bundle adjustment, while producing significantly richer 3D structures and improved geometric accuracy.

</details>


### [154] [DSAQuant: Denoising-Stage-Aligned Quantization-Aware Training for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04031)
*Shuaiting Li,Zelin Gao,Haibin Shen,Yujun Shen,Haotong Qin,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DSAQuant，一个针对视频扩散模型(VDM)的与去噪阶段对齐的量化感知训练框架。作者发现传统量化方法在不同时间步长上统一处理，忽视了视频去噪的分阶段特性（早期建立全局结构、中后期细化局部细节），导致量化后模型视觉细节和纹理严重退化。DSAQuant在训练时使用去噪阶段导向监督，早期保留教师蒸馏以稳定结构规划，后期转向目标驱动优化增强细节重建；推理时通过去噪阶段门控引导，在最后去噪步骤禁用CFG以防止量化误差被放大为高频伪影。在Wan和CogVideoX系列上W4A4和W3A3设置下，该方法相较SOTA基线在VBench平均分上最高提升6.60。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型(VDM)虽在文生视频上取得显著进展，但高内存和计算成本阻碍实际部署。量化感知训练(QAT)是有效的压缩加速方案，但现有QAT方法在VDM中面临独特挑战：虽然能保留提示语义、全局布局和粗略运动，但量化模型严重退化视觉细节、纹理保真度和清晰度。作者将这种退化追溯到传统量化pipeline的时间步长无关设计，其忽视了视频去噪的阶段级功能。

Method: DSAQuant包含两个核心设计：1) 训练阶段的Denoising-Stage Oriented Supervision——在去噪早期步骤保留教师蒸馏以维持稳定结构规划，在后期步骤转向目标驱动优化以增强细节重建；2) 推理阶段的Denoising-Stage Gated Guidance——在最后去噪步骤禁用CFG(classifier-free guidance)，防止其将量化引入的误差放大为高频伪影。

Result: 在Wan和CogVideoX系列模型上，分别于W4A4和W3A3量化设置下进行实验。DSAQuant一致性地优于SOTA QAT基线，在激进的W3A3量化下VBench平均分最高提升6.60，同时保持强大的文本-视频对齐能力。

Conclusion: 有效的VDM量化不仅需要降低量化误差，还需要将量化训练和推理与视频扩散的阶段级特性对齐。实验结果证明，将去噪阶段的认知融入QAT设计，能显著缓解量化带来的细节退化问题，在压缩模型的同时保持高质量的视频生成效果。

Abstract: Video diffusion models (VDMs) have achieved impressive progress in text-to-video generation, but their high memory and computational costs hinder practical deployment. Quantization-aware training (QAT) is an effective solution for compressing and accelerating advanced generative models without runtime overhead at inference. However, existing QAT methods suffer from a distinctive challenge in VDMs: while they often preserve prompt semantics, global layout, and coarse motion, the quantized model severely degrades visual details, texture fidelity, and sharpness. In this paper, we trace this degradation to the timestep-agnostic design of conventional quantization pipelines, which overlooks the stage-wise functionality of video denoising. In VDMs, early denoising steps mainly establish global structure and motion, whereas middle and late steps refine local appearance and high-frequency details. Based on this insight, we propose DSAQuant, a Denoising-Stage-Aligned Quantization-aware training framework for VDMs. During training, Denoising-Stage Oriented Supervision preserves teacher distillation in early steps for stable structure planning, while shifting later steps toward target-driven optimization to enhance detail reconstruction. During inference, Denoising-Stage Gated Guidance disables CFG in the final denoising steps to prevent it from amplifying quantization-induced errors into high-frequency artifacts. Extensive experiments on the Wan and CogVideoX families under W4A4 and W3A3 settings show that DSAQuant consistently outperforms the SOTA QAT baseline, improving the VBench average score by up to 6.60 under aggressive W3A3 quantization while preserving strong text-video alignment. These results demonstrate that effective VDM quantization requires not only reducing quantization error, but also aligning quantization training and inference with the stage-wise nature of video diffusion.

</details>


### [155] [Editable Visual Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04034)
*Junyan Ye,Wei Liu,Dongzhi Jiang,Zichen Wen,HaoDong Li,Zhutao Lv,Jiaxin Lin,Jinhua Yu,Jun He,Zilong Huang,Rui Chen,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"可编辑视觉设计"（Editable Visual Design）新范式，由编码智能体驱动：VLM作为"创意大脑"负责需求理解、任务规划与审美判断，图像生成模型作为"视觉世界模拟器"按需合成独立视觉资产。系统在"先想象、后行动"的闭环工作流下生成隔离资产、编写原生HTML/CSS并依据渲染反馈迭代优化，最终交付具有解耦图层与真实文本的可编辑产物，支持用户在GUI上进行直观拖拽与布局调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散基座模型（如GPT-Image-2和Nano-Banana）虽然视觉表现力强，但端到端生成本质上是扁平位图且文字易出错，无法进行逐层后期编辑；而基于编码的视觉生成方式虽能提供精确布局控制和分层解耦，却缺乏全局审美直觉且编码复杂视觉资产困难。两类方法各有局限，亟需一种兼顾美学效果与可编辑性的新范式。

Method: 提出Editable Visual Design范式，由Coding Agent驱动。将VLM设为"创意大脑"负责需求理解、任务规划与审美评判，图像生成模型作为按需调用的"视觉世界模拟器"合成独立视觉资产。系统遵循"先想象后行动"闭环流程：先生成隔离资产，再编写原生HTML/CSS，最后基于视觉渲染反馈进行迭代优化。另有Agent Design Replay机制用于忠实复现类似专业人类设计师的创作与推理轨迹。

Result: 在海报、信息图及其他场景上的验证表明，该范式成功实现了精致美学与生产级可编辑性的双重目标，能够交付具备解耦图层和真实文本的可编辑设计产物。

Conclusion: 可编辑视觉设计范式有效弥合了扩散模型与代码生成方法之间的鸿沟，在保持优美视觉呈现的同时赋予设计产物真正可编辑性，为用户提供直观的界面操作体验，为视觉设计生成开辟了新方向。

Abstract: While diffusion base models such as GPT-Image-2 and Nano-Banana exhibit remarkable visual expressiveness, their end-to-end generation inherently yields flattened bitmaps with error-prone text, precluding layer-wise post-editing. Conversely, code-based visual generation via Coding Agents provides precise layout control and decoupled layers, yet remains constrained by a lack of global aesthetic intuition and the difficulty of coding complex visual assets.
  To address this, we propose Editable Visual Design, a new paradigm driven by a Coding Agent. We designate the VLM as the ``creative brain'' for requirement comprehension, task planning, and aesthetic judgment, while utilizing the image generation model as an on-demand ``visual world simulator'' to synthesize standalone visual assets. Operating under an ``imagine first, then act'' closed-loop workflow, the agent generates isolated assets, writes native HTML/CSS, and iteratively refines the design against visual rendering feedback.
  Furthermore, Agent Design Replay faithfully reproduces the creative and reasoning trajectory akin to that of professional human designers. Ultimately, the system delivers editable artifacts with decoupled layers and real text, enabling users to perform intuitive mouse dragging and layout adjustments on a graphical user interface. Validations on posters, infographics, and other scenarios show that this paradigm successfully achieves both refined aesthetics and production-grade editability.

</details>


### [156] [Continuous Actions from Discrete Minds: Latent-Aligned Planning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04070)
*Ruoyu Yao,Yusen Xie,Qingzhao Liu,Pei Liu,Zewei Yang,Yipeng Zhu,Xiaolong Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LaPla是一个统一的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)框架，通过潜在对齐规划将语义理解与运动执行无缝衔接。它利用基于残差VQ-VAE的动作分词器捕获车辆动力学，并将连续潜在表示(而非离散码本)映射到预训练潜在空间，经冻结解码器直接生成物理可行的轨迹，消除了量化误差并绕过自回归生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型(基于离散推理)与自动驾驶(需要连续、物理约束的运动执行)之间存在显著的模式鸿沟。现有的VLA方法通常依赖离散码本查找来规划动作，不可避免地引入量化误差，且在推理时需要耗时的自回归生成。

Method: 1) 设计基于残差VQ-VAE的动作分词器，将车辆运动学与轨迹特征编码到结构化潜在空间；2) 将该表示作为物理先验而非离散码本查找，用于弥合高维语义与原始动作空间之间的模态差距；3) 输入多视角图像、历史动作和文本指令，通过并发动作查询在单次前向传播中因果关注多模态上下文；4) 将隐藏状态直接投影到预训练VQ-VAE潜在空间，由冻结解码器转换为动作，消除量化误差并确保轨迹的物理可行性。

Result: 在nuScenes基准上，LaPla实现了竞争性的开环性能，长时域L2误差比最先进的VLA方法降低15.52%。在NVIDIA AlpaSim模拟器的闭环评估中，成功率提升33.34个百分点，且推理延迟显著降低。

Conclusion: LaPla通过将离散VLA推理与连续、物理约束的自动驾驶执行桥接，以潜在对齐规划为核心，同时提升了规划的物理合理性、闭环驾驶成功率与推理效率。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between the discrete reasoning of Vision-Language Models and the continuous, physics-constrained nature of autonomous driving remains a significant challenge. In this work, we introduce LaPla, a unified Vision-Language-Action (VLA) framework featuring latent-aligned planning to seamlessly ground semantic understanding in precise motion execution. We first design an action tokenizer based on a residual vector-quantized variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE), capturing vehicle kinematics and encoding trajectory features into a structured latent space. Rather than discrete codebook lookups that inevitably introduce quantization errors, LaPla repurposes this representation as a physical prior to bridge the modality gap between high-dimensional semantics and the raw action space. Specifically, given multimodal inputs integrating multi-view images, historical actions, and textual instructions, LaPla incorporates concurrent action queries to causally attend to the multimodal context in a single forward pass, projecting hidden states directly into the pretrained VQ-VAE latent space. The frozen decoder then translates these continuous latents into actions, effectively eliminating quantization errors and ensuring physically plausible trajectories while bypassing time-consuming autoregressive generation. Extensive experiments on the nuScenes benchmark demonstrate that LaPla achieves competitive open-loop performance, reducing long-horizon L2 error by 15.52% compared to state-of-the-art VLA methods. Closed-loop evaluations on the NVIDIA AlpaSim simulator further confirm its superior capability in ensuring smooth driving progress, improving the success rate by 33.34 percentage points with significantly reduced inference latency.

</details>


### [157] [TAP-Path: Task-Adaptive Structural and Token Pruning for Efficient and Trustworthy Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04071)
*Mehedi Hasan,Ashfak Yeafi,Md Khairul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAP-Path，一种面向病理学基础模型的任务自适应压缩框架，直接在预训练的Virchow2编码器上进行结构精简而非蒸馏到独立学生网络。通过保留32个transformer块中的24个并剪枝70%的patch tokens，编码器参数减少24.96%（631.24M降至473.70M），计算量减少35.20%（340.13G降至220.40G FLOPs）。在32类组织病理学基准上达到87.98%准确率，优于完整Virchow2（86.89%）和UNI2-h（87.67%），并在433例CPTAC外部样本上保持91.22%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理学基础模型虽然提升了表示学习的可迁移性，但近年来性能提升往往依赖拥有数亿参数和极高推理成本的大型编码器，需要在不显著损失性能的前提下降低模型规模与计算开销。

Method: TAP-Path不将预训练Virchow2编码器蒸馏为独立的学生模型，而是直接对其重构。方法包括：基于验证集的transformer块选择、物理移除冗余块、输入自适应的patch token剪枝、多深度特征恢复、以及轻量化的门控任务头。此外还引入了一种基于验证集的稀有类别感知目标函数用于次要操作分析。

Result: 最终模型保留32个transformer块中的24个和70%的patch tokens，编码器参数量减少24.96%、计算量减少35.20%。在32类基准上，三种任务头优化种子下测试准确率87.98±0.067%，平衡准确率81.26±0.49%，macro-F1 82.38±0.48%，Brier得分0.1800±0.0005，失败检测AUROC为0.9047±0.0060。外部CPTAC 433例评测获得91.22%准确率和91.10%平衡准确率。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的结构性与token稀疏化方法能够在保持可靠性的前提下，改善大型病理学基础模型的精度-效率权衡，且其优势在内部基准和外部评测中均得到验证。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models improve transferable representation learning for histopathology, but recent gains often rely on encoders with hundreds of millions of parameters and high inference cost. We propose TAP-Path, a task-adaptive compression framework that directly restructures a pretrained Virchow2 encoder rather than distilling it into a separate student. TAP-Path combines validation-driven transformer-block selection, physical removal of redundant blocks, input-adaptive patch-token pruning, multi-depth feature recovery, and a lightweight gated task head. The final model retains 24 of 32 transformer blocks and 70% of patch tokens after pruning, reducing encoder parameters by 24.96% (631.24M to 473.70M) and analytical encoder compute by 35.20% (340.13G to 220.40G FLOPs). Across three task-head optimization seeds, TAP-Path achieved $87.98 \pm 0.067%$ test accuracy, $81.26 \pm 0.49%$ balanced accuracy, and $82.38 \pm 0.48%$ macro-F1 on a 32-class histopathology benchmark, compared with 86.89% for full Virchow2 and 87.67% for UNI2-h. TAP-Path achieved a Brier score of $0.1800 \pm 0.0005$ and failure-detection AUROC of $0.9047 \pm 0.0060$. A validation-only rare-aware objective improved rare-class balanced accuracy in a secondary operating analysis. Frozen external evaluation on 433 CPTAC samples yielded $91.22 \pm 0.83%$ accuracy and $91.10 \pm 0.81%$ balanced accuracy. These results show that task-adaptive structural and token sparsification can improve the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of large pathology foundation models while preserving reliability under internal and external evaluation.

</details>


### [158] [CORE: Improving Compositional Reasoning in MLLM Embedding via Reranker Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04083)
*Tingyu Song,Mingxin Li,Yanzhao Zhang,Dingkun Long,Chu Liu,Pengjun Xie,Yilun Zhao,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CORE方法，利用跨注意力重排器（reranker）的结构化判断来蒸馏嵌入模型，使其具备组合检索能力。通过合成跨越五个组合匹配级别的候选列表，并引入Rank-KL目标函数训练嵌入模型复现重排器的细粒度排序，在COLA、SUGARCREPE++、NEGBENCH等多个基准上取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MLLM的嵌入模型在组合检索任务中表现受限，难以区分包含相同概念但属性-对象绑定不同的场景。然而同一骨干网络作为跨注意力重排器时却能解决这类区分，这激发了将重排器的组合判断能力蒸馏到嵌入模型中的动机。

Method: 提出CORE框架：(1) 合成跨越五个组合匹配级别的候选列表作为训练数据；(2) 引入Rank-KL目标函数，训练嵌入模型复现重排器对候选列表的细粒度排序；(3) 在同一数据与调参预算下，对比对比学习、成对CoSENT和列表式Rank-KL三种训练策略。

Result: Rank-KL总体性能最强。CORE-RERANKER-8B在三个组合推理基准上达到82.7%总平均，超过Jina-Reranker 10.7个百分点；CORE-EMBED-8B在嵌入模型中取得最佳总平均(0.666)。改进可迁移至MCMR基准，且不牺牲COCO和Flickr30K上的检索性能。

Conclusion: 实验表明CoSENT和Rank-KL比对比学习能更有效利用多级别监督信号，列表式Rank-KL蒸馏策略是让嵌入模型习得重排器组合判断能力的最优方式，且该改进具备良好的泛化性与检索性能保持性。

Abstract: MLLM-based embedding models remain limited in compositional retrieval, often failing to distinguish scenes containing the same concepts but different attribute-object bindings. Yet the same backbone can resolve such distinctions when used as a cross-attentive reranker, motivating us to distill its compositional judgments into the embedding model. We propose CORE, which synthesizes candidate lists spanning five compositional matching levels and introduces a Rank-KL objective that trains the embedding model to reproduce the reranker's fine-grained ranking. We further introduce a graded evaluation protocol and compare contrastive learning, pairwise CoSENT, and listwise Rank-KL under the same data and tuning budget. Our comparison shows that both CoSENT and Rank-KL use the multi-level supervision more effectively than contrastive learning, with Rank-KL achieving the strongest overall performance. Across three compositional reasoning benchmarks (COLA, SUGARCREPE++, NEGBENCH), CORE-RERANKER-8B achieves an 82.7% total average, outperforming Jina-Reranker by 10.7 points, while CORE-EMBED-8B achieves the best total average (0.666) among all evaluated embedding models. The improvements transfer to the MCMR benchmark without sacrificing retrieval performance on COCO and Flickr30K.

</details>


### [159] [Efficient Semantic Understanding from Digital Foveation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04088)
*Caterina Caccavella,Vittorio Fra,Andreas Ziegler,Giulia D'Angelo,Yulia Sandamirskaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受生物视觉启发的数字中央凹感知轻量级主动视觉管线，通过显著性驱动的注视点选择、高分辨率中央凹观察和低分辨率上下文信息实现稀疏语义理解。在ADE20K-Object数据集上，单次中央凹观察仅需4.7%的计算成本即可达到基线95.9%的Top-1和96.9%的Top-3精度，语义累积在58.6%计算开销下恢复90.6%的物体召回率，证明了选择性计算分配是均匀密集处理的高效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的密集语义分割在整幅图像上均匀分配计算资源，不考虑场景复杂度或任务相关性，造成大量计算浪费。受生物视觉启发，作者探究能否通过数字中央凹感知，以更高效的方式实现语义理解，并呼吁使用超越传统像素级分割指标的评估协议。

Method: 构建了一个轻量级主动视觉管线，包含四个核心组件：显著性驱动的注视点选择、高分辨率中央凹观测、低分辨率上下文信息整合、语义累积与自适应计算。评估上除了传统密集预测指标外，还引入物体级评估来度量稀疏观测下的语义理解能力。

Result: 在ADE20K-Object数据集上，单次中央凹观测以4.7%的计算成本达到基线95.9%的Top-1精度和96.9%的Top-3精度；在场景级别，语义累积以58.6%的计算量恢复了基线90.6%的物体召回率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在选择性分配计算资源时，大量的语义理解可以从稀疏观测中涌现。主动视觉可作为均匀密集处理的高效替代方案，同时凸显了超越传统逐像素分割指标的新评估协议的必要性。

Abstract: Dense semantic segmentation allocates computational resources uniformly across the entire image, regardless of scene complexity or task relevance. Inspired by biological vision, we investigate whether semantic understanding can be achieved more efficiently through digital foveated perception. We introduce a lightweight active-vision pipeline that combines saliency-driven fixation selection, high-resolution foveal observations, low-resolution contextual information, semantic accumulation, and adaptive computation. Beyond conventional dense prediction metrics, we use object-level evaluation to measure semantic understanding under sparse observations. On ADE20K-Object, a single foveated observation achieves 95.9% of the baseline Top-1 accuracy and 96.9% of the baseline Top-3 accuracy while requiring only 4.7% of the computational cost. At the scene level, semantic accumulation recovers 90.6% of the baseline object recall while using 58.6% of the computation. These results suggest that substantial semantic understanding can emerge from sparse observations when computation is allocated selectively, highlighting active vision as an efficient alternative to uniform dense processing and motivating evaluation protocols beyond conventional pixel-wise segmentation metrics.

</details>


### [160] [The Shape of Time: Video-Token Contrast for Temporal Understanding in VideoLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04110)
*Yumeng Shi,Quanyu Long,Yin Wu,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VT-Contrast提出了一种面向视频语言模型（VideoLM）的表示层时间反事实学习目标。论文指出，现有VideoLM按顺序接收视频帧，但主要监督信号作用于生成的文本而非视频token表示，导致模型可借助物体、场景和语言先验等捷径回答时间问题，而无需内部视频表示真正捕捉事件进展。VT-Contrast通过监督选定晚期层的最后一帧视频token，并用按Kendall tau距离排序的同一视频重排反事实样本与保序视角进行对比学习，无需架构改动即可集成到多种VideoLM训练任务中，在时间理解基准上取得整体性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序观看帧并不代表模型真的理解了时间。现代VideoLM虽然接收有序视频流，但其主要监督作用于生成的文本而非视频token表示——而事件动态恰恰应首先在视频token表示中涌现。这种错位使模型能通过物体、场景和语言先验等捷径推断时间答案，内部视频表示无需真正捕捉事件进展，导致模型缺乏对真实时间因果关系的理解能力。

Method: 提出VT-Contrast，一种表示级别的时间反事实训练目标。核心设计回答两个问题：(1) 时间监督应对表示层的何处起作用——选择晚期层的最后一个视频帧token，在该位置期望时间信息在语言生成前完成整合；(2) 应暴露何种时间差异——将保序视图与同一视频的重排反事实视图进行对比，并按Kendall tau距离对反事实的混乱程度分级。该方法无需架构改动，且兼容多种VideoLM训练任务。

Result: VT-Contrast在多个时间理解基准上整体提升了VideoLM的性能，验证了表示层时间反事实监督的有效性。

Conclusion: VT-Contrast证明，仅监督输出文本不足以让VideoLM学到真正的时间表示；通过在视频token表示层注入区分保序与乱序视图的时间反事实信号，可以促使模型内部表示捕捉事件进展，从而在不改动架构的前提下增强时间理解能力。

Abstract: Seeing frames in order does not mean representing time. Modern VideoLMs receive ordered video streams, yet their main supervision acts on generated text rather than video-token representations where event dynamics should first emerge. This mismatch allows models to learn temporal answers from shortcuts such as objects, scenes, and language priors, without requiring internal video representations to capture event progression. To address this, we propose VT-Contrast, a representation-level temporal counterfactual objective for VideoLMs. Its design asks where temporal supervision should act and what temporal differences it should expose. VT-Contrast supervises selected late-layer last-frame video tokens, where temporal information is expected to be integrated before language generation, and contrasts order-preserving views with same-video reordered counterfactuals graded by Kendall tau distance. It requires no architectural changes, is compatible with diverse VideoLM training tasks, and improves overall performance across temporal understanding benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/ANDgate99/VT-Contrast.

</details>


### [161] [BooM-VVT: Boosting Mask-Free Video Virtual Try-On with Image-Level Pseudo Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04120)
*Wei Zhang,Xin Li,Peishu Shi,Jialin Gao,Xuekang Peng,Zhichao Lian,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BooM-VVT，一种基于关键帧驱动的无掩码视频虚拟试穿框架。通过多阶段训练策略利用图像级伪数据进行无掩码定位学习，减少视频级伪数据需求；提出服装敏感关键帧采样和帧共享3D-RoPE以提升服装一致性；构建OmniView大规模多视角试穿数据集以支持复杂视角下的可靠试穿视频生成。实验证明其在时间一致性和服装保真度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法依赖掩码进行试穿区域定位，对大运动和严重遮挡鲁棒性差；无掩码图像试穿虽有效但扩展到视频时因视频级伪数据构建成本过高而困难；此外，粗糙的关键帧采样和多视角试穿数据稀缺限制了现有方法在服装一致性和多样试穿任务上的表现。

Method: BooM-VVT包含三个核心组件：(1)多阶段训练策略，利用廉价的图像级伪数据实现无掩码定位学习；(2)服装敏感关键帧采样（Garment-Sensitive Keyframe Sampling），依据与服装相关的人体区域选取关键帧以捕捉服装外观；(3)帧共享3D-RoPE（Frame-Shared 3D-RoPE），在关键帧与目标视频帧间建立时空对应以准确迁移服装细节。另构建OmniView多视角试穿数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明BooM-VVT在时间一致性和服装保真度方面均优于现有方法，有效支持复杂相机视角下的可靠试穿视频生成及多种试穿任务。

Conclusion: BooM-VVT通过无掩码定位、服装敏感关键帧采样、帧共享3D-RoPE及多视角数据集四项创新，解决了视频虚拟试穿中掩码依赖、服装不一致性和数据稀缺等关键问题，为领域内后续工作提供了新范式与基础资源。

Abstract: Video virtual try-on (VVT) aims to generate realistic videos of a person wearing a target garment. Recent methods leverage a keyframe-driven video generation paradigm to improve in-the-wild performance, yet they still rely on masks to localize try-on regions, making them vulnerable to large motions and severe occlusions. Although mask-free image-based try-on methods have shown promising results by leveraging large-scale pseudo data, extending this paradigm to videos remains difficult, as constructing video-level pseudo data is prohibitively expensive. Furthermore, coarse keyframe sampling and the scarcity of multi-view try-on data limit existing keyframe-driven methods in maintaining garment consistency and handling diverse try-on tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose BooM-VVT, a mask-free VVT framework built upon the keyframe-driven paradigm. To achieve mask-free VVT, we introduce a multi-stage training strategy that leverages image-level pseudo data for mask-free localization learning, substantially reducing the need for costly video-level pseudo data. To improve garment consistency, we propose Garment-Sensitive Keyframe Sampling, which selects keyframes based on garment-relevant body regions to better capture garment appearance. We further introduce Frame-Shared 3D-RoPE to establish spatiotemporal correspondences between keyframes and target video frames for accurate garment-detail transfer. Finally, we construct OmniView, a large-scale multi-view try-on dataset to support reliable try-on video generation under complex camera viewpoints and diverse try-on tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BooM-VVT achieves superior temporal consistency and garment fidelity over existing methods. Project page: https://boomvvt.github.io/boomvvt.

</details>


### [162] [Beyond Retrieval: Progressive Latent Memory Evolution for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04131)
*Hongyu Qu,Guangming Yao,Ling Xing,Xiaobin Hu,Rongxing Ding,Guibin Zhang,Fan Zhang,Yi Yuan,Xiangbo Shu,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentStream，一种渐进式潜在工作记忆框架，将流式视频理解的记忆范式从"存储-检索"转变为"检索-内化"，通过三级层次化记忆组织、层次化潜在记忆演化与置信度引导的优化，实现视频历史信息内化为紧凑的固定长度潜在记忆，并在多模态视频基准上取得新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解方法采用"存储-检索"（store-and-retrieve）范式，将历史证据保留为外部视觉上下文，阻止其被内化为紧凑且不断演化的潜在记忆，从而无法持续引导流式推理。这种外部记忆机制限制了模型在严格因果性和有界内存条件下对长视频历史的有效利用。

Method: LatentStream包含三个协同组件：(1) 查询无关的层次化流式记忆（Query-agnostic Hierarchical Streaming Memory），通过Jenks引导的自适应整合将视觉历史组织为短/中/长期三个层级，在固定内存预算下运行；(2) 层次化潜在记忆演化（Hierarchical Latent Memory Evolution），为潜在记忆token分配逐步扩大的记忆感受野，使其能从对应范围内迭代检索历史证据并内化为紧凑的固定长度潜在记忆；(3) 渐进式置信度引导的潜在记忆优化（Progressive Confidence-guided Latent Memory Optimization），基于分组预测熵构建层次化进度奖励，联合优化潜在记忆token与检索证据，促进越来越自信的流式推理。

Result: 大量实验证明LatentStream在现有的在线和离线视频基准上均取得了新的最先进（SOTA）结果。

Conclusion: 通过将流式记忆从"存储-检索"转向"检索-内化"，LatentStream验证了将历史视觉证据内部化为紧控潜在记忆的可行性，为严格因果性与有界内存约束下的流式视频理解提供了更优的记忆表征范式。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to process continuous visual inputs and respond to user queries under strict causality and bounded memory. Existing approaches typically compress historical observations into an external memory bank and retrieve query-relevant evidence as additional visual context. Though effective, this store-and-retrieve paradigm keeps historical evidence as external visual context, preventing it from being internalized into a compact, evolving latent memory that can continuously guide streaming reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce LatentStream, a progressive latent working memory framework that shifts streaming memory from store-and-retrieve to retrieve-and-internalize. Specifically, LatentStream comprises three coordinated components. First, Query-agnostic Hierarchical Streaming Memory organizes visual history into short-, mid-, and long-term levels under a fixed memory budget through Jenks-guided adaptive consolidation. Once a query arrives, Hierarchical Latent Memory Evolution equips groups of latent memory tokens with progressively expanding memory receptive fields, enabling them to iteratively retrieve historical evidence from their corresponding scopes and internalize it into a compact, fixed-length latent memory. Finally, Progressive Confidence-guided Latent Memory Optimization constructs a hierarchical progression reward from group-wise predictive entropy and jointly refines the latent memory tokens and retrieved evidence, encouraging increasingly confident streaming reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LatentStream achieves new state-of-the-art results on existing online and offline video benchmarks.

</details>


### [163] [Persistent Identity Preservation in Generative Image Models: A Benchmark and Evaluation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04151)
*Mengwei Ren,Xuaner Zhang,Zhihao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统对比了三种主体驱动生成范式中身份保持的差异（输入上下文、可训练参数LoRA、持久身份层PHOTA IDENTITY），发现当前生成式基础模型的图像质量与指令遵循能力并不必然带来可靠的身份保真度，而持久身份表示方法在生成、编辑、修复及多主体场景中均能显著降低身份漂移，并提出身份可作为与底层生成模型独立组合的持久主体知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式图像模型在图像质量、复杂指令遵循和精确编辑方面已取得显著进展，但在生成或编辑具体主体的图像时，身份保真度仍然不足——当姿态、表情、外观、视角或场景变化时身份会发生漂移。现有主体驱动方法在"身份在哪里表示"这一核心问题上做出了根本不同的选择，但缺乏系统性的基准测试与对比，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文对三种主流的主体驱动范式——基于输入上下文的方式（如GPT-Image-2、NB2）、基于可训练主体特定模型参数的方式（LoRA）以及可跨代际和编辑复用的持久身份层（PHOTA IDENTITY）——进行了系统基准测试。评估覆盖主体驱动生成、编辑、修复及多主体场景，并设计了逐步加重身份保持压力的任务（如迭代编辑、小尺度主体、严重图像退化、多主体组合）。

Result: 实验发现：1）身份保持仍是当前生成式基础模型的明显短板，图像质量与指令遵循能力并不必然意味着强身份保真度；2）身份退化在迭代编辑、小主体尺度、严重图像退化和多主体组合条件下更为突出；3）持久身份方法能显著降低生成、编辑和修复过程中的身份退化，在不同基础模型上均一致地改善身份保持，同时保持相当的指令遵循能力和感知图像质量。

Conclusion: 身份保真并不能仅从日益强大的生成式模型中自然涌现，而是可以被表示为与底层生成模型独立组合的持久主体知识。这一发现对主体驱动图像生成领域的设计范式具有重要启示：身份作为一种可复用的持久表示，应被显式建模而非依赖生成模型隐式涌现。

Abstract: Generative image models can now produce high-quality images, follow complex instructions, and support precise edits, but they still struggle to preserve who or what is being depicted. When generating or editing images of a specific subject, identity may drift as the pose, expression, appearance, viewpoint, or surrounding scene changes. Existing subject-driven methods make fundamentally different choices about where identity is represented: through the input context (GPT-Image-2, NB2), as trainable subject-specific model parameters (LoRA), or as a persistent identity layer (PHOTA IDENTITY) reusable across generations and edits. We systematically benchmark these paradigms across subject-driven generation, editing, restoration, and multi-subject settings, with tasks designed to increasingly stress identity preservation. Our results show that identity preservation remains a distinct limitation of current generative foundation models: strong image quality and instruction following do not necessarily imply strong identity fidelity, and identity degradation becomes more pronounced under iterative edits, small subject scales, severe image degradation, and multi-subject composition. Persistent identity substantially reduces this degradation across generation, editing, and restoration, consistently improving identity preservation when applied to different foundation models while maintaining comparable instruction adherence and perceptual image quality. These results suggest that identity does not simply emerge from increasingly capable generative models, but can instead be represented as persistent subject knowledge that is composed independently with the underlying generative model.

</details>


### [164] [Zero-Shot Novel Depth Synthesis Using 3D Foundation Models Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04174)
*Denis M. Akola,David F. Fouhey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Z3D方法，利用3D基础模型（如VGGT）的内部表征，通过潜在扩散模型从新视角解码出点图和深度图，从而推断场景中的3D结构，包括隐藏表面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D基础模型在3D视觉任务上表现出色，但其内部表征是否包含足够的通用3D场景知识以支持从新视角推断3D结构（包括隐藏表面）尚不明确。作者假设这些模型为完成3D重建任务必然学习到了关于3D场景的大量通用知识。

Method: 首先证明可以从3DFM的内部表征中解码隐藏表面；在此基础上提出Z3D，该方法对3DFM表征执行潜在扩散，从而估计未见视图中的点图（pointmaps），进而生成新视图的深度图。

Result: 实验表明Z3D能够跨多个数据集预测新视图的真实深度图，验证了3DFM内部表征确实编码了丰富的通用3D场景知识。

Conclusion: 3DFM的内部表征包含足够的3D场景知识，可通过潜在扩散解码出新视角下的3D结构，这为利用预训练3D基础模型进行视图外推和三维场景理解提供了新的思路。

Abstract: 3D Foundation Models (3DFMs) such as VGGT have recently pushed the boundaries of 3D vision by predicting rich unified representations with feed-foward transformers. The scene representations learned by these models enable strong performance on multiple 3D vision tasks. In this paper, we investigate using their internal representations to infer 3D in the scene from new views. Our hypothesis is that in order to solve the task of 3D reconstruction, these models need to learn a representation that includes a large amount of general knowledge about 3D scenes. After showing that it is possible to decode hidden surfaces from internal 3DFM representations, we propose a method, Z3D, that estimates pointmaps in unseen views by doing latent diffusion on 3DFM representation. We show that Z3D can predict realistic depth maps for new views across multiple datasets.

</details>


### [165] [Seeing Before Synthesizing: VLM-Guided Transition Event Discovery for Weakly-Supervised Dense Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04183)
*Ye-Chan Kim,Seunghee Choi,SeungJu Cha,Si-Woo Kim,Hwiseon Kim,Hyungee Kim,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SBS框架，通过VLM生成视觉锚定的帧级叙事并检测语义变化点，用于弱监督稠密视频描述中的过渡事件定位与生成，在ActivityNet Captions和YouCook2上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督稠密视频描述方法利用LLM合成的辅助过渡字幕，但这些字幕缺乏视觉锚定且被刚性分配到所有事件间间隙的固定位置和时长，导致视觉-语言对齐不准确。

Method: SBS框架包含两步：首先利用VLM为事件间间隙生成帧级叙事，并基于语义变化检测过渡点；对于识别出的过渡，通过融合时间中点和语义变化点来细化时间掩码，并选择最大化视觉-语言对齐的宽度。

Result: 在ActivityNet Captions和YouCook2两个基准上，SBS在描述生成和事件定位两个任务上均取得最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过先"看"再"合成"的方式，自适应地仅在语义变化明显的间隙提供视觉锚定的语言引导，有效解决了合成字幕缺乏视觉基础的问题。

Abstract: Weakly-Supervised Dense Video Captioning aims to localize and describe multiple events in untrimmed videos given only an ordered set of event-level captions per video. Recent work synthesizes auxiliary transition captions via LLM to provide additional vision-language alignment, but these captions lack visual grounding and are rigidly assigned to every inter-event gap at a fixed location and duration. To address these, we propose Seeing Before Synthesizing (SBS), a framework that adaptively provides visually grounded linguistic guidance only where warranted. Leveraging a VLM, we generate frame-level narratives for the inter-event gaps and detect transitions from the semantic variation across them. For identified transitions, we then refine inter-event temporal masks by blending the temporal midpoint with the semantic change point and selecting the width that maximizes vision-language alignment. Experiments on ActivityNet Captions and YouCook2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both captioning and localization.

</details>


### [166] [One Editor, Many Edits: A Unified Training-Free Framework for Diverse Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04190)
*Adheesh Sunil Juvekar,Onkar Kishor Susladkar,Kiet A. Nguyen,Muntasir Wahed,Nabeel Bashir,Xiaona Zhou,Tianjiao Yu,Vedant Shah,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditVid是一个无需训练的视频编辑框架，通过稀疏因果记忆、对应关系驱动的注意力后标记注入和软潜在混合三种机制，统一支持指令引导和参考引导（主题引导）的视频编辑，在FiVE基准上取得78.16的准确率，大幅超越最强的无需训练基线（58.95）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编辑涉及多种编辑范式，但目前难以在一个统一框架内同时实现高质量、多样化的指令引导和主题引导编辑，现有方法往往局限于单一编辑模式，缺乏兼具局部连贯性、长程身份保持和编辑局部性的统一解决方案。

Method: EditVid采用无需训练（training-free）的框架，包含三个核心组件：1）稀疏因果记忆机制用于保持局部时序连贯性；2）基于对应关系的注意力后标记注入，用于长程身份保持；3）软潜在混合技术以确保编辑的局部性（仅修改目标区域）。该统一框架同时支持指令引导和参考引导的编辑任务。

Result: 在FiVE基准上，EditVid达到78.16的FiVE-Acc，远超最强的无需训练基线（58.95）；在IVEBench上取得有竞争力的结果；用户研究显示EditVid相对于7种竞争方法获得51.8%的总体偏好率。

Conclusion: EditVid通过三种互补机制的巧妙组合，在无需训练的前提下实现了覆盖多种编辑范式的统一视频编辑框架，验证了在一个系统中同时支持指令引导和主题引导编辑的可行性，并在多个基准上展现了显著优势。

Abstract: Video editing spans diverse editing paradigms, yet achieving high-quality instruction-guided and subject-guided editing within a single unified framework remains challenging. We introduce EditVid, a training-free framework combining sparse causal memory for local coherence, correspondence-based post-attention token injection for long-range identity preservation, and soft latent blending for edit locality. The same framework supports instruction-guided and reference-guided edits, including style transfer, attribute modification, object insertion, part-level editing, and subject replacement. On FiVE, EditVid achieves 78.16 FiVE-Acc, compared with 58.95 for the strongest evaluated training-free baseline, while obtaining competitive results on IVEBench. A user study further shows a 51.8\% overall preference for EditVid over 7 competing methods.

</details>


### [167] [Puffin-World: Scaling a Unified Multimodal Model with Native 3D World States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04196)
*Kang Liao,Yihang Luo,Xiao-Ming Wu,Linyi Jin,Size Wu,Chunyu Lin,Yao Zhao,Fei Wang,Wei Li,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Puffin-World，一个统一的多模态架构，将物理理解、空间模拟与3D世界生成/重建整合于一体，无需依赖外部离线模块。联合建模物理（重力场与纬度）、几何（深度）和外观（图像）三种原生世界状态，采用统一的Omni-Camera表示，并通过跨未来帧传播物理动力学。配套构建Puffin-16M数据集（1500万视觉-语言-相机三元组及100万轨迹），并开源代码、模型与数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D世界生成与重建方法通常依赖多个独立的离线模块进行物理模拟、空间推理和视觉生成，缺乏统一的架构来协同完成物理理解、空间模拟与3D世界构建，难以支持需要多任务协同的闭环应用。

Method: 1) 联合建模三种原生世界状态：物理（重力场和纬度）、几何（深度）与外观（图像）；2) 提出统一的Omni-Camera表示，支持多样化任务与灵活相机运动；3) 引入跨未来帧物理动力学传播策略，将绝对相机属性锚定于真实世界以获得物理一致且视觉稳定的生成；4) 在单一生成过程中耦合外观与几何，联合合成每帧未来视图并重建其底层几何；5) 构建Puffin-16M数据集用于规模化训练。

Result: 该方法支持交错式闭环应用（如模仿与自校准的世界探索），在需要多种任务协同的场景下表现出色，实现了物理一致且视觉稳定的世界生成与几何重建。

Conclusion: Puffin-World展示了一种无需外部模块的统一多模态架构范式，可同时完成物理理解、空间模拟与3D生成重建；通过耦合外观与几何、传播物理动力学，为闭环世界探索等复杂应用提供了可行的技术路径。作者已开源代码、模型与数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: We propose Puffin-World, a unified multimodal architecture that integrates physical understanding, spatial simulation, and 3D world generation and reconstruction without relying on external offline modules. To reliably construct and interact with 3D worlds, our framework jointly models three native world states: physics (gravity field and latitude), geometry (depth), and appearance (image), together with a unified Omni-Camera representation that supports diverse tasks and flexible motions. Beyond modeling these states, we introduce a strategy for propagating physical dynamics across future frames. By grounding absolute camera properties in the real world, Puffin-World enables physically consistent and visually stable world generation. We further couple appearance and geometry within a single generative process, jointly synthesizing each future view and reconstructing its underlying geometry. This unified paradigm enables interleaved closed-loop applications requiring synergy across multiple tasks, including mimic and self-calibrated world exploration. To scale Puffin-World to complex scenarios, we construct Puffin-16M, comprising 15 million vision-language-camera triplets and 1 million trajectories featuring various and challenging motions. To foster further research in this area, we released the code, models, and datasets.

</details>


### [168] [Principia: Relational Physics Tests for Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04200)
*Varun Varma Thozhiyoor,Shivam Tripathi,Venkatesh Babu Radhakrishnan,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Principia基准，通过配对物体的关系一致性来评估视频模型中的牛顿物理推理能力，该指标与标定无关。在八个物理现象上测试了六个SOTA视频生成器和多个视觉语言模型，结果显示视频生成器在Principia上均不超过0.42（尽管VBench得分约0.8），最佳VLM检测物理违例的准确率仅67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频模型中的物理推理评估困难，因为绝对运动测量依赖于帧率、物体尺度和相机标定，这些在生成视频中通常不明确或不可用。现有基准（如VBench）评分高但无法反映真实的物理一致性。

Method: 提出Principia基准，利用同一场景中遵循相同物理规律的两个物体的运动必须满足可预测的关系这一特性，实现标定无关的物理评估。涵盖八个物理现象（重力、恢复系数、摩擦、转动惯量、抛体运动、动量、摆、弹簧振荡），涉及平移、旋转、碰撞和振荡四类动力学；提出图像空间中直接量化的标定无关一致性分数。使用受控协议录制的真实场景进行测试。

Result: 评估了六个SOTA视频生成器的数千次生成，所有模型在Principia上的得分均不超过0.42，尽管它们在VBench上得分约0.8。视觉语言模型在检测关系物理违例方面，最佳模型仅达67%准确率，多数模型接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 现有视频生成器虽然在常用基准（如VBench）上表现良好，但在Principia揭示的物理关系一致性方面存在严重缺陷；视觉语言模型检测物理违例的能力同样不足。这指明了视频生成与理解领域在物理推理方面的重大差距。

Abstract: Evaluating physical reasoning in video models is difficult because absolute motion measurements depend on frame rate, object scale, and camera calibration, all of which are often ambiguous or unavailable in generated video. We propose a different approach. When two objects in the same scene obey the same physical law, their motions must satisfy predictable relationships, and these relationships hold independent of calibration. We introduce Principia, a benchmark that evaluates Newtonian physics through relational consistency between paired objects. Principia spans eight phenomena - gravity, restitution, friction, rotational inertia, projectile motion, momentum, pendulum, and mass-spring oscillation - across translational, rotational, collisional, and oscillatory dynamics, using real-world scenes recorded under controlled protocols. We also introduce a calibration-independent consistency score that quantifies physical violation directly in image space. Across thousands of generations from six state-of-the-art video generators, no model exceeds 0.42 on Principia despite all scoring around 0.8 on VBench. Vision-language models are evaluated on their ability to detect relational physics violations, with the best model achieving only 67% accuracy and most performing near chance level.

</details>


### [169] [Scal3R: Learning Efficient Multi-Relative Pose Query for Scalable Online 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04201)
*Chin-Yang Lin,Yang-Che Sun,Cheng Sun,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yen-Yu Lin,Wei-Chen Chiu,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Scal3R，一种用于在线 3D 重建的新方法。该方法通过多层参考相对姿态查询，将轻量级可学习 token（约占参数 1%）通过非对称注意力注入完全冻结的骨干网络，并配合在线位姿图优化与回环检测抑制长程漂移。相比在线基线，在 KITTI 上平均 ATE 降低 60% 以上，且在 Virtual KITTI、Sintel、TUM-Dynamic、ScanNet 和 7-Scenes 等多个基准上达到最先进性能，单 GPU 上 8 小时即可收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 3D 重建模型在处理长视频时表现不佳，原因是姿态相对于固定首帧锚点回归会导致外推远超训练分布，微小漂移累积并被放大为严重的几何坍缩。然而作者观察到各帧深度保持稳定，说明局部几何完好而全局姿态头失效，这种解耦性启发了本文方法。

Method: Scal3R 将在线重建重构为多层参考相对姿态查询问题。使用约占总参数 1% 的轻量级可学习 token，通过非对称注意力注入完全冻结的骨干网络；姿态相对于多个历史关键帧进行查询。再配合在线位姿图优化系统与回环检测来抑制长程漂移。

Result: Scal3R 在单个 GPU 上 8 小时即可收敛；相比在线基线，在 KITTI 上平均 ATE 降低超过 60%；并在 Virtual KITTI、Sintel、TUM-Dynamic、ScanNet 和 7-Scenes 等数据集上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦稳定的深度估计与易崩溃的全局姿态回归，Scal3R 有效解决了长视频下在线 3D 重建的漂移与几何坍缩问题，在效率与精度上均具有显著优势。

Abstract: Online 3D reconstruction models perform poorly on long videos. This happens because regressing poses relative to a fixed first-frame anchor forces extrapolation far beyond the training distribution. Small drifts accumulate and amplify into significant geometric collapse. However, we observe that per-frame depth remains stable throughout this failure. The backbone's local geometry remains intact; only the global pose head breaks down. Motivated by this decoupling, we introduce Scal3R. This approach reformulates online reconstruction as multi-reference relative pose querying. We use lightweight learnable tokens, which make up about ~1% of the parameters, and inject them into a completely frozen backbone via asymmetric attention. This setup queries poses relative to multiple past keyframes. An online pose-graph optimization system with loop closure suppresses long-range drift. Scal3R reaches convergence in 8 hours on a single GPU. It reduces the average ATE by over 60% on KITTI compared to the online baseline. It also achieves state-of-the-art performance across Virtual KITTI, Sintel, TUM-Dynamic, ScanNet, and 7-Scenes. Project page: https://linjohnss.github.io/scal3r/

</details>


### [170] [TokenMatch: 3D Mesh Correspondence Transformer with Curvature-Guided Tokenisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04202)
*Adeela Islam,Zorah Lähner,Vittorio Murino,Vladislav Golyanik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TokenMatch是一种基于Transformer的统一模型，用于3D形状对应估计。它通过自适应曲率引导的网格标记化、自注意力和交叉注意力机制，在部分到部分及完整形状匹配上均达到最优性能，且推理速度达到亚秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习驱动的3D形状对应估计方法在部分观测和非等距变形下表现不佳；已有方法依赖手工特征或模板表示，而基于functional maps的生成模型推理成本高、可解释性差、泛化能力弱。

Method: TokenMatch采用前馈式Transformer架构，包含自注意力和交叉注意力机制以学习patch级和点级关系及稠密对应。核心创新在于根据曲率引导将网格自适应token化为patch，从而学习形状特定的几何描述子。模型仅在BeCoS部分到部分数据集上训练，无需重新训练或微调即可泛化到完整形状匹配。

Result: 在CP2P、PSMAL、BeCoS、FAUST、SCAPE和SHREC'19等多个标准基准上评估，TokenMatch在平均测地误差和交并比指标上大多数情况下优于现有方法，同时推理速度更快，达到亚秒级。

Conclusion: TokenMatch通过Transformer和自适应曲率引导的网格token化，成功解决了非等距部分到部分形状匹配的挑战，实现了对完整与部分形状匹配的统一、高效且强泛化能力的解决方案。

Abstract: While data-driven 3D shape correspondence estimation has recently seen substantial progress, robust matching under partial observations and strong non-isometric deformations remains challenging. Existing learning-based approaches often rely on hand-crafted descriptors or template-based representations, whereas recent generative models over functional maps suffer from high inference cost, limited interpretability, and poor generalisation to partial shapes. In response to these limitations, this paper introduces TokenMatch, a new transformer-based unified model for estimating 3D shape correspondences. Our feed-forward approach trained exclusively on BeCoS, a challenging non-isometric partial-to-partial shape-matching dataset, can generalise to matching full shapes without retraining or fine-tuning. TokenMatch uses self- and cross-attention mechanisms to efficiently learn patch-level and point-level relations as well as dense correspondences between shape pairs. Our core insight is that meshes can be adaptively tokenised into patches using shape curvature guidance, enabling effective learning of shape-specific geometric descriptors for correspondence estimation. We evaluate TokenMatch on standard benchmarks for partial and full shape matching, including CP2P, PSMAL, BeCoS, FAUST, SCAPE, and SHREC'19. Our method achieves consistently high performance, in most cases outperforming existing methods for partial and full shape matching in the mean geodesic error and intersection-over-union metrics, while also running faster at sub-second inference speeds.

</details>


### [171] [Temporal Self-Distillation: Learning Visual State Tracking in Videos Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04203)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Wenshuai Zhao,Mohammad Hassan Vali,Arno Solin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出S³T（Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time），一种完全自包含的连续视频状态追踪框架，通过将时间采样密度视为privileged information，利用稠密视角的动态蒸馏来训练稀疏视角学生模型进行自监督学习，无需任何标签、独立教师或奖励信号，且不增加推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频状态追踪方法依赖人工标注或外部信号，且先前的自进化方法（self-evolving）对状态追踪能力的提升十分有限，无法有效捕捉视频中的连续动态状态。作者假设视频同一剪辑中更稠密的时间采样能更准确地恢复运行状态，并据此设计一种完全自包含的学习范式，以突破现有方法在标签依赖和性能瓶颈上的限制。

Method: S³T将时间采样密度视为特权信息（privileged information）：denser视图（更稠密采样）作为教师，sparser视图（更稀疏采样）的学生模型共享相同权重，学习匹配教师的next-token分布。模型自行生成目标，因此不需要外部标签、独立教师网络或奖励信号。在LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B上进行训练，结合模型souping和视觉编码器适配等策略增强效果。

Result: 在LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B上，S³T在VSTAT上将准确率提升+1.74（单模型），+2.38（模型souping），+2.70（结合额外视觉编码器适配）；从无标签合成剪辑学到的能力可迁移至真实视频，在VSTAT-YouTube状态追踪问题上提升+7.95，在MVBench Action Count上提升+4.50；而先前的自进化方法对状态追踪几乎无提升。

Conclusion: S³T是首个完全自包含的连续视频状态追踪自蒸馏框架，证明了"时间采样密度作为特权信息"这一假设的有效性，能以无标签、零额外推理代价的方式显著提升视频状态追踪性能，且合成数据上学到的能力可有效泛化至真实视频场景。

Abstract: We introduce S$^3$T (Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully self-contained framework for continuous video state tracking. Our method treats temporal sampling density as privileged information, based on the hypothesis that a denser view of the same clip recovers the running state more accurately. This view serves as the teacher, while a sparse-view student with the same weights learns to match its next-token distribution. The model generates its own target, so training requires no labels, separate teacher, or reward signal, and adds no inference cost. On LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B, S$^3$T improves VSTAT accuracy by $+1.74$ as a single model, $+2.38$ with souping, and $+2.70$ with additional vision-encoder adaptation, while prior self-evolving methods leave state tracking largely unchanged. The capability learned from unlabeled synthetic clips transfers to real videos, improving performance by $+7.95$ on VSTAT-YouTube state-tracking questions and $+4.50$ on MVBench Action Count.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [A Public-Key-Dependent Adversarial-Deletion Ceiling for Fixed-Alphabet Multi-Bit Pseudorandom Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02943)
*Frederick Dehmel,Shilun Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明对于固定字母表上的多消息公钥伪随机码（PRC），不存在能够抵抗所有依赖于公钥和码字的对抗性删除信道的方案，当删除率超过1-γ_q^{LCS}时（其中γ_q^{LCS}是两个独立均匀q元字符串的渐近归一化最长公共子序列长度）。对于q=2，该阈值约为0.207334008。该证明扩展到列表解码场景，阈值随消息数趋近于1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪随机码（PRC）是一种将纠错码与伪随机性结合的密码学原语。此前对PRC在删除信道下的鲁棒性研究主要针对公钥独立的或特定删除模式的信道，尚不清楚是否存在能够抵抗"公钥相关且码字相关"的对抗性删除信道的多消息公钥PRC。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，通过信息论方法证明此类鲁棒性的不可能性上界。

Method: 作者利用最长公共子序列（LCS）的渐近性质进行碰撞论证。核心思想是：如果存在一个对δ-删除信道鲁棒的多消息公钥PRC，则两个独立采样的码字都必须以高概率解码到各自的源消息。由于删除信道可以依赖公钥和码字，攻击者可以构建一个信道，使得该信道对一个码字是"友好的"（可解码）而对另一个码字是"灾难性的"。通过LCS界限，证明当δ>1-γ_q^{LCS}时，两个独立码字之间存在足够长的公共子序列，使得可以用信息论方法构造信道同时混淆两种码字，而伪随机性仅用于将均匀字符串上的LCS事件转移到独立采样的码字上。列表解码部分使用γ_2^{(m)}（m个均匀二元字符串的渐近归一化最长公共子序列长度）进行推广。

Result: 1) 对任意固定q≥2，不存在多消息公钥PRC（单输出解码器）能抵抗所有删除率为δ>1-γ_q^{LCS}的依赖公钥的对抗性删除信道。2) 对q=2，当前最优下界γ_2≥0.792665992给出不可达阈值δ>0.207334008。3) 扩展到列表解码：对任何固定列表大小L，若消息空间至少含L+1个消息，则不存在输出列表大小至多L的此类PRC在δ>1-γ_2^{(L+1)}下鲁棒。由于γ_2^{(m)}=1/2+Θ(1/√m)，该界限趋近于1/2。4) 证明本质上是信息论的，不需要密钥，且此结果不适用于 oblivious 编辑信道（公钥独立的信道）。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了公钥PRC在公钥相关对抗性删除信道下鲁棒性的基本不可能性边界。对二元字母表，当删除率超过约0.207时，不存在任何多消息公钥PRC（即使允许相对较小的列表解码，阈值也随列表增大而趋近于0.5删除率下的不可能性扩展）。这些结果明确了公钥PRC鲁棒性的固有局限，表明要实现更强鲁棒性需要依赖 oblivious 信道模型或其他安全性假设。

Abstract: A pseudorandom code (PRC) is a keyed error-correcting code whose codewords are computationally indistinguishable from uniform strings. We study public-key PRCs over fixed alphabets against adversarial deletions, where the deletion channel may both depend on the public encoding key and the transmitted codeword.
  Let $γ_q^{\mathrm{LCS}}$ denote the asymptotic normalised longest-common-subsequence length of two independent uniform $q$-ary strings. We prove that for every fixed $q\ge2$ no multi-message public-key PRC with a single-output decoder is robust against all such $δ$-deletion channels for any $δ>1-γ_q^{\mathrm{LCS}}$. For $q=2$, the current rigorous bound $γ_2\ge0.792665992$ rules out every constant $δ>0.207334008$. The proof uses pseudorandomness only to transfer an LCS event from uniform strings to independently sampled codewords and therefore also the resulting collision argument is information-theoretic and requires no secret key. We also extend the argument to list decoding: for every fixed constant $L$, provided the message space contains at least $L+1$ messages, no such PRC with output lists of size at most $L$ is robust for $δ>1-γ_2^{(L+1)}$. Since $γ_2^{(m)}=1/2+Θ(1/\sqrt m)$, these thresholds approach $1/2$. Our bounds are specific to public-key-dependent adversarial channels and do not apply to oblivious edit channels.

</details>


### [173] [Privacy-Preserving Heterogeneous Multi-LLM Federated Inference for Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02947)
*Yagna Manasa Boyapati,Chong Yu,Tianyu Jiang,Justin Zhan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个联邦推理框架，允许多个商业LLM API在不访问原始学生数据或模型内部结构的情况下协作进行认知诊断。通过本地拉普拉斯噪声实现ε-局部差分隐私，并用残差聚合减轻模型异构性，在三个教育基准上的实验表明强隐私保障与极小的准确性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的教育系统在平衡隐私保护与准确认知诊断之间仍面临重大挑战，现有方法难以在不泄漏原始学生数据或不暴露专有模型内部结构的情况下实现多模型协作诊断。

Method: 提出联邦推理框架，采用异构多LLM架构（LLaMA-3.3-70B、GPT-4o-mini、Claude-3-Haiku等联邦实体）。各实体的预测输出在本地添加拉普拉斯噪声以实现ε-局部差分隐私后再进行聚合，同时采用基于残差的聚合机制来缓解模型异构性。框架建立在诚实的但好奇的信任范式上，API提供者假定不会滥用查询，而差分隐私机制保护发布的诊断结果免受外部推理。

Result: 严格的隐私-效用分析表明框架提供强隐私保障且准确性损失极小，跨三个教育基准的真实世界评估证实了框架的实用性和跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了隐私保护与认知诊断准确性之间的平衡，为AI教育系统在多商业LLM协作场景下提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Significant challenges remain in AI-driven educational systems in balancing privacy preservation with accurate cognitive diagnosis. To overcome this, we propose a federated inference framework in which several commercial LLM APIs collaborate without requiring access to raw student data or proprietary model internals. Using multiple federated entities, such as LLaMA-3.3-70B, GPT-4o-mini, and Claude-3-Haiku, our framework builds upon a heterogeneous multi-LLM architecture. The predictions generated by these entities are combined with epsilon-local differential privacy by adding Laplace noise locally to each entity's prediction output before aggregation, while residual-based aggregation mitigates model heterogeneity. Our approach is predicated on an honest-but-curious trust paradigm in which API providers are presumed not to abuse submitted queries, and our differential privacy mechanism shields the published diagnostic results from external inference. We conduct rigorous privacy-utility analysis showing strong privacy guarantees with minimal accuracy loss, and extensive real-world evaluations across three educational benchmarks confirm the framework's practical usability and cross-domain generalizability.

</details>


### [174] [PrivateHub: Contrastive Diffusion Model for Private Sensor-Intensive Environment Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02958)
*Jiechao Gao,Yuandong Pan,Jie Wang,Michael Lepech,Bradford Campbell*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Privatehub利用扩散模型中的对比学习生成合成多传感器数据流，在保持非隐私应用可检测的同时隐藏隐私应用，将隐私应用推断准确率降低40-50%而不损害非隐私性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器密集型环境通过异构数据流推断用户应用实现智能服务，但并非所有应用都应暴露，用户希望某些活动保持隐私。现有方法（如差分隐私和基于规则的过滤）仅保护单个数据流，无法应对跨传感器推断带来的隐私风险，因此需要一种能处理多传感器间联合推断隐私泄露的新方法。

Method: Privatehub包含两个阶段：1) App-Conditioned Pre-training (ACP)，在应用嵌入条件下以多传感器数据训练扩散模型；2) App-Aware Fine-tuning (AAF)，通过对比学习将隐私应用与非隐私应用的数据分离。此外还定义了多传感器共享场景下的威胁模型。

Result: 在三个真实世界多传感器数据集上的实验表明，Privatehub能将隐私应用的推断准确率降低40-50%，同时不损害非隐私应用的性能，并且在攻击者基于合成数据重新训练时仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Privatehub通过扩散模型与对比学习相结合，有效解决了跨传感器推断带来的隐私保护问题，为多传感器共享环境下的数据隐私保护提供了一种新的可行方案。

Abstract: Sensor-intensive environments enable many intelligent services by inferring user applications from heterogeneous data streams. However, not all applications should be exposed: users want some activities to stay private. This creates a tension between inferring applications for useful services and preventing unwanted inference. Existing approaches such as differential privacy and rule-based filtering protect individual streams but cannot address the privacy risk from cross-sensor inference.
  We introduce Privatehub, which uses contrastive learning within a diffusion model to generate synthetic multi-sensor streams that keep non-private applications detectable while concealing private ones. Privatehub has two stages: App-Conditioned Pre-training (ACP), which conditions the model on multi-sensor data with application embeddings, and App-Aware Fine-tuning (AAF), which separates private from non-private data via contrastive learning. We also define a threat model for the multi-sensor sharing setting. Experiments on three real-world multi-sensor datasets show Privatehub lowers private-application accuracy by 40 to 50\% without hurting non-private performance, and stays robust when the attacker retrains on the synthetic data.

</details>


### [175] [When Optimization Becomes Manipulation: Defending Generative Search against Malicious Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02964)
*Haozhang Li,Yangguang Shao,Xinjie Lin,Zhong Guan,Mi Zhou,Junzheng Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GEO Defender，一种面向生成式搜索引擎的两阶段无需微调的防御框架，通过Shield Reranker与自然语言经验库（TFSG）显著降低恶意GEO攻击的成功率（从50.32%降至6.20%），同时保持良性证据使用率和答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式搜索引擎面临恶意生成式引擎优化（GEO）的攻击威胁，该类攻击通过重写网页文档以操纵生成答案中的引用偏好。现有防御手段（事实校验、困惑度过滤等）无法有效应对，因为攻击文档与原文在事实上保持一致，且其放大的特征与高质量良性内容高度相似。因此亟需一种能够有效区分GEO重写文档与良性文档、且无需对目标LLM进行微调的防御方案。

Method: GEO Defender采用与攻击链对齐的两阶段防御架构：1）Shield Reranker：在冻结的基础重排序器之上学习基于偏好的防御残差，降低GEO重写文档的排名，同时保留相关性判断；2）Training-Free Shield Generation (TFSG)：将防御结果提炼为自然语言经验库，在推理阶段引导目标LLM的源文档使用决策。整个过程无需对目标LLM进行微调。

Result: 在两个SOTA闭源LLM和三个开源LLM上、跨越七种GEO攻击的实验中，GEO Defender将平均攻击成功率从50.32%降至6.20%，保留了94.12%的良性证据使用率，答案质量得以保持，并能从构建实例泛化到未见过的攻击类型。

Conclusion: GEO Defender通过两阶段防御有效抵御GEO攻击，在不微调目标LLM的前提下实现高防御成功率与良性内容保留率，且具备对未知攻击的泛化能力，为生成式搜索引擎的安全性提供了一种实用且可扩展的防御路径。

Abstract: This paper focuses on defending generative search engines against malicious Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), which rewrites web documents to match engines' citation preferences and thereby manipulates generated answers. Recent GEO methods have advanced from hand-crafted rewriting to automated and agentic optimization, substantially increasing the visibility of target documents in generated answers. However, defending against such manipulation poses two major challenges: attack documents remain factually consistent with their originals, rendering fact verification and perplexity filtering ineffective, and the features they amplify equally characterize high-quality benign content. To address these limitations, we propose GEO Defender, a two-stage defense aligned with the attack chain that requires no fine-tuning of the target LLM. GEO Defender consists of Shield Reranker and Training-Free Shield Generation (TFSG). Specifically, Shield Reranker learns a preference-based defensive residual over a frozen base reranker, demoting GEO-rewritten documents while preserving relevance judgments, and TFSG distills defense outcomes into a natural-language experience library that guides the target LLM's source use at inference. Experiments on two state-of-the-art closed-source LLMs and three open-source LLMs across seven GEO attacks demonstrate that GEO Defender reduces the average attack success rate from 50.32% to 6.20%, retains 94.12% of benign-evidence use, preserves answer quality, and generalizes to unseen attacks from construction instances.

</details>


### [176] [Privacy-Preserving Topology-Guided Safety for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems via Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02967)
*Jinxi Yu,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Levina Li,Dong Liu,Zhi Zhang,Wenxiao Zhao,Yanxuan Yu,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出FGLGuard，一种基于图联邦学习的隐私保护型多智能体系统（MAS）安全防护方法。各运营商在本地使用带边特征的图注意力网络检测风险代理，仅共享模型更新而不共享私有数据，通过近端局部目标、域平衡聚合、校准阈值等方法实现跨域安全防护，在多个基准上超越集中式上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于拓扑的安全防护方法（训练GNN定位风险代理并干预拓扑）假设单一运营商可汇聚所有带标签轨迹。但跨组织场景下该假设不成立：各组织的轨迹包含私有提示词、工具输出和专有工作流，且单一数据孤岛无法看到完整的攻击分布，因此需要隐私保护的联邦安全防护方案。

Method: 将隐私保护型MAS安全防护建模为图联邦学习，实例化为FGLGuard。每个运营商在自有评判标记的代理通信图文上训练带边特征的图注意力检测器，仅共享模型更新。方法包含四个核心组件：面向非IID客户端的近端局部目标函数、域平衡聚合策略、约束过度拒绝的阈值校准方法、以及针对被阻止答案的加固改写机制。此外还有上游协同评分机制。

Result: 在Agent-SafetyBench、R-Judge和AgentDojo三个基准上，联邦FGLGuard在完全不汇聚数据的情况下超越了域内集中式上限。跨四个不同域运营商联邦训练后，与多域集中式方法仅差0.03 AUROC，而任何单域防护在其它域全部失效。离线集成方案在分布偏移下崩溃（AUROC仅在域内重训后从0.51提升到0.70），难以直接部署。在线FGLGuard将AgentDojo的真实攻击成功率降低43%，同时几乎不损失效用、零API成本和可忽略的能力损失。

Conclusion: 联邦学习在MAS安全防护中并非可选而是必要：防护必须适配各站点的私有轨迹数据。FGLGuard通过图联邦学习实现了在不汇聚任何原始数据的情况下，取得优于集中式上限的跨域安全防护性能，为隐私敏感的多组织协作场景提供了可行的MAS防护范式。

Abstract: Topology-guided safeguards for LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) train a GNN over the inter-agent communication graph to localize risky agents and intervene on the topology---but they assume one operator can pool all labeled traces. Across organizations that assumption breaks: episodes contain private prompts, tool outputs, and proprietary workflows, and no silo alone sees the full attack distribution. We cast privacy-preserving MAS safeguarding as graph federated learning and instantiate FGLGuard: each operator fits an edge-featured graph attention detector on its own judge-labeled episode graphs and shares only model updates. The method couples a proximal local objective for non-IID clients, domain-balanced aggregation, over-refusal-constrained threshold calibration, corroborated upstream scoring, and a guarded rewrite for blocked answers. Federation is not optional: off-the-shelf transfer collapses under distribution shift (AUROC 0.51 to 0.70 only after in-domain retraining), so a deployable guard must adapt on each site's private traces. On Agent-SafetyBench, R-Judge, and AgentDojo, federated FGLGuard exceeds the in-domain centralized ceiling on all three benchmarks without pooling any data---where unsupervised anomaly guards and local-only training fail. One guard federated across four different-domain operators comes within 0.03 AUROC of multi-domain centralization, while any single-domain guard collapses on the others. Live FGLGuard cuts AgentDojo's ground-truth attack-success rate by 43% at near-unguarded utility, zero API cost, and negligible capability loss.

</details>


### [177] [Privacy Leakage in Federated Learning: Gradient-Based Client Identity Inference and Defenses for Inertial Sensing in Vehicular Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02971)
*Ali Akarma,Toqeer Ali Syed,Muhammad Khan,Qurat-ul-ain Mastoi,Adeel Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究车联网联邦学习（FL）中的客户端身份推断攻击：诚实但好奇的服务器可从客户端上传的权重增量中近乎完美地（准确率≈1.000）识别出数据来源客户端。该问题在UCI HAR数据集（作为车辆IMU数据的代理）上得到验证。作者提出轻量级"裁剪-加噪"（clip-then-noise）防御方案，实验表明在sigma∈[0.1,0.2]的实用区间内可将攻击准确率降至接近随机水平，同时FL精度损失低于5%，并通过Renyi差分隐私核算提供形式化(ε,δ)-DP保证。此外，集成FL（Ensemble FL）通过1/K匿名集下界提供结构性隐私保护且无噪声开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然联邦学习被广泛视为车联网中保护隐私的训练范式，但客户端上传的模型更新仍会泄露可识别客户端身份的信息。这破坏了安全关键型V2X应用所依赖的匿名性，并加剧了对抗性ML、模型投毒和后门攻击的现有担忧。作者旨在揭示并量化解 决这一此前未被充分研究的具体身份识别风险，同时探索兼顾隐私与效用的可行防御方案。

Method: 以UCI Human Activity Recognition (HAR)基准作为车载IMU数据流的易获取代理，从服务器视角利用惯性（IMU）测量数据所对应的权重增量进行客户端身份推断。测试五种攻击分类器和五种非IID数据划分，评估诚实但好奇服务器恢复客户端身份的能力。防御端采用固定裁剪(C=1.0)下扫描高斯噪声标准差sigma∈{0.00,0.05,0.10,0.20,0.50,1.00}的轻量级clip-then-noise机制，通过Renyi差分隐私核算报告形式化(ε,δ)-DP预算；同时评估集成FL的结构性隐私优势（1/K匿名集边界）。实验采用SHA-256加密的train/evaluation梯度不相交验证、三种随机种子及计数归一化的攻击者优势指标。

Result: 在未防御的情况下，五种攻击分类器在五种非IID划分上均能以近完美的准确率（约1.000）恢复客户端身份，证实了明确的身份可识别风险。在防御方面，sigma∈[0.1,0.2]的实用噪声区间可将攻击准确率降至接近随机水平，而联邦学习相对精度损失低于5%，表明存在协同良好的隐私-效用平衡区域。集成FL提供与噪声机制正交且无精度代价的结构性隐私增益。

Conclusion: 车联网FL客户端上传的权重增量构成可被服务器端利用的明确身份识别泄漏，即使在看似隐私保护的FL框架下匿名性也并非自然保证。轻量级噪声注入（裁剪后加高斯噪声）配合Renyi-DP核算可在实用范围内有效抵御此类攻击，且对FL精度的代价可控。集成FL可作为补充性结构防御。作者明确将HAR定位为代理数据集，并提出在真实车载遥测数据上验证所需的扩展条件，强调结论向实际V2X场景迁移时需要进一步验证。

Abstract: As vehicular networks move toward 5G/6G edge intelligence, federated learning (FL) is widely promoted as a privacy-preserving way for vehicles and infrastructure to train shared models without exposing raw sensor data. Yet the updates clients transmit still leak enough information to identify who sent them, which threatens the anonymity that safety-critical V2X applications assume and adds to existing concerns over adversarial ML, model poisoning, and backdoor attacks. We study server-side client identity inference from transmitted weight deltas using inertial (IMU) measurements, evaluated on the UCI Human Activity Recognition (HAR) benchmark as an accessible proxy for the IMU streams produced onboard connected vehicles. Across five attack classifiers and five non-IID partitions, an honest-but-curious server recovers client identity with near-perfect accuracy (approximately 1.000) from undefended updates, confirming a concrete identifiability risk. We then quantify the privacy-utility trade-off of a lightweight clip-then-noise defense by sweeping Gaussian noise (sigma in {0.00, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50, 1.00}) at fixed clipping (C=1.0), and report formal (epsilon, delta)-DP budgets through Renyi accounting. A practical region (sigma in [0.1, 0.2]) drives attack accuracy to near-random while costing under 5% relative FL accuracy. Ensemble FL supplies complementary structural privacy with a 1/K anonymity-set bound and no noise penalty. Results are supported by cryptographic (SHA-256) train/evaluation gradient disjointness, three seeds, and a count-normalized attacker-advantage metric. We position HAR explicitly as a proxy and discuss what validation on true vehicular telemetry would require.

</details>


### [178] [Boundary-Mutation Testing for Pattern-Based Secret Detection: A Rule-Level Method and Cross-Scanner Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02983)
*Shweta Mishra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出边界变异测试（boundary-mutation testing）方法，用于系统性地改变模式化密钥扫描器的上下文变量，通过从规则自身正则表达式生成凭据、嵌入真实源代码上下文，并在规则层面而非工具层面进行分类，得出三种检测指标。在含43条规则的开源扫描器、Gitleaks 8.21.2和TruffleHog 3.82.13上的测试中，主检测对象在十个上下文中的检出率保持≥0.9976，但当凭据以连字符结尾时降至0.5233，涉及五条规则。作者提出修复方案并验证其恢复完全鲁棒性且无新增误报，同时报告一个值得警惕的现象：一个看似合理的初步修复方案使两条规则静默退化。Gitleaks存在无关的硬编码终止符白名单缺陷，TruffleHog无边界脆弱性但覆盖最窄。研究采用边际而非条件失败概率，比较单位为十种凭据类型而非数百个样本，最终报告空结果而非排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模式的密钥扫描器通常使用基于示例的fixtures进行验证，但这类方法只固定单个变量（凭据周围的文本），无法系统地探测扫描器在真实世界上下文变化下的鲁棒性缺陷。作者旨在引入一种更系统、更严谨的测试方法，以发现被传统验证方法遗漏的边缘情况和边界缺陷。

Method: 提出边界变异测试框架：(1) 从每个检测规则的自身正则表达式生成合法凭据；(2) 将凭据嵌入多种逼真的源代码上下文中；(3) 在规则层面而非整个工具层面进行结果分类；(4) 定义三种检测指标；(5) 对三个代表性扫描器进行对照实验并计算边际失败概率；(6) 针对发现的缺陷提出修复并设计验证流程（包括重新运行同一测试集以捕捉静默回归）。

Result: 主扫描器在十个上下文下检出率≥0.9976，但凭据以连字符结尾时骤降至0.5233。受影响的有五条规则：两条固定计数量词规则完全且确定性失败；三条可变计数量词规则发生回溯并匹配截断凭据；熵回退机制挽救了部分失败但降低了严重性等级。Gitleaks存在源确认的硬编码终止符白名单缺陷（导致大多数凭据类型的完全漏检）；TruffleHog无边界脆弱性但覆盖范围最窄。一种常见令牌格式结构上免疫，另一种64次中失败1次。在292,527行真实代码上，误报排序相对合成语料发生反转。因按类型检测是确定的，比较单位为十种凭据类型，无召回差异达到显著性，因此报告无效结果。

Conclusion: 边界变异测试能有效暴露传统示例式验证无法发现的扫描器缺陷；凭据边界的句法结构（尤其是量词类型对边界字符的处理方式）对检出率有显著影响；修复方案可恢复完全鲁棒性且不引入新误报，但需警惕表面合理实则引入静默回归的修复方案，重新运行完整测试集对验证修复安全至关重要；边际失败概率比条件概率更适合作为上报指标；在无显著差异时应报告空结果而非排序。

Abstract: Pattern-based secret scanners are commonly validated with example-based fixtures that fix one variable: the text surrounding a credential. We introduce boundary-mutation testing to vary that context, generating credentials from each rule's own regular expression, embedding them in realistic source contexts, and classifying outcomes at the rule level rather than the tool level, yielding three detection metrics. Applied to three scanners - a 43-rule open-source scanner, Gitleaks 8.21.2, and TruffleHog 3.82.13 - detection in the primary subject holds at >=0.9976 across ten contexts but collapses to 0.5233 when a credential ends in a hyphen. Five rules are affected: two, with fixed-count quantifiers, fail totally and deterministically; three, with variable-count quantifiers, backtrack and match a truncated credential; an entropy fallback rescues some failures but downgrades their severity. We validate a repair restoring full robustness with no new false positives, and report a caution: a plausible first attempt silently regressed two rules, caught only by re-running the same battery. Gitleaks has an unrelated, source-confirmed defect - a hard-coded terminator allowlist causing total misses for most credential types - while TruffleHog shows no boundary fragility but narrowest coverage. We report marginal, not conditional, failure probabilities: one common token format is structurally immune, another fails once in 64; on 292,527 lines of real code, the false-positive ordering inverts relative to the synthetic corpus. Because per-type detection is deterministic, the comparison unit is ten credential types, not hundreds of samples; no recall difference reaches significance, so we report the null result, not a ranking.

</details>
